HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B008","root_001122/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0c56dec79022cd5c61b3","text":"4. **The heart as the final material in a consuming chamber.** Fixed `ٱلْأَفْـِٔدَةِ` activates `ف ء د B001`, heat, fire, and implements or places for roasting and baking, alongside its direct heart branch `B002`. Earlier `ٱلْحُطَمَةُ` activates `ح ط م B008`, a grinder or digester, and later `مُّؤْصَدَةٌ` activates `و ص د B001`, a chamber sealed shut. The packet gives processing, heat, hearts, and closure; I infer an oven-like coordination without claiming that any single word literally names an oven. The line can thus read materially: the process does not stop at breaking the exterior but subjects the inward center to sustained transformation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_370cf6b1d7693454f3ba","text":"1. **Fire as investigator of the inward account.** Evidence: `ط ل ع B003` (overlooking/disclosing a matter) and `ف ء د B002` (heart as inner kindling), activated by `ح س ب B002` and `خ ل د B004` in 104:3. Structural cue: the fire named in 104:6 is immediately given an action toward أفئدة. Assigned role: تطلع supplies disclosure; أفئدة supplies the hidden seat. Abductive move: the fire is directed toward where the false reckoning settled. Reading change: the ayah reads less as mere burning and more as inner audit.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001122/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4497154b5b1359287da9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:7:4\",\"root_001122\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Başkalarına sözle yöneltilen itme ve darbeler, çıktıkları merkeze dönerek saldırganın yüreğini vurur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4a92ec49b4cceb26356e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:7:2\",\"root_000945\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Ateşin, örtülü işaret ve niyetleri izleyip kaynağına ulaşan bir öncü gibi davrandığı ileri sürülür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B003","root_001122/B002","root_001672/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_53d465cf461d535a1f16","text":"1. **Thermal penetration as disclosure of the interior.** Fixed `تَطَّلِعُ` activates `ط ل ع B003`, overlooking, gaining access to, and disclosing an inward matter; fixed `ٱلْأَفْـِٔدَةِ` realizes `ف ء د B002`, the heart viewed as inner kindling. The prior kindled fire, `و ق د B001`, supplies heat, while the relative clause gives that heat an inward object. The packet supplies movement onto the hearts; I infer that reaching them also reveals the settled orientations within. The line therefore reads as diagnostic penetration: outer fire finds and discloses an already heat-like inner center.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B006","root_001122/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_681dfe9bccbe4dfee87d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:7:2\",\"root_000945\",[\"B006\"]],[\"104:7:4\",\"root_001122\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ateş yüzeyde kalmaz; yüreği belirlenmiş son hedef yapan tek yönlü bir giriş yolu izler ve sonraki kapanma geri çıkışı keser.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6e28714a62e45aed0ae7","text":"3. **Scouting the covert gesture.** Evidence: `ط ل ع B004` (scouting vanguard) and `د ر ي B002/B003` (aiming, stealthy pursuit), activated by `ل م ز B001` in 104:1. Structural cue: the surah moves from covert social signaling to a fire that \"comes upon\" the heart. Assigned role: تطلع is the scout/approach that reaches what concealed speech tried to hide. Abductive move: the only adequate answer to hidden لمز is a disclosure that can track the hidden source. Reading change: the ayah makes judgment a search operation, not only a penalty.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001122/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_989e9b5dfb178dd733af","text":"4. **The heart struck by the violence it sent outward.** Evidence: `ف ء د B003` (heart struck/afflicted), activated by `ه م ز B003` and `ل م ز B002` (prodding, pushing, striking) and `ح ط م B001`. Structural cue: outward pressures at 104:1 culminate in an inward target. Assigned role: فؤاد receives the returned force. Abductive move: the social blows dealt through speech are redirected to the center from which they came. Reading change: the ayah turns the punishment inward: the offender's heart becomes the struck object.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B003","root_001122/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9bd0c83128c796c9a7f6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:7:2\",\"root_000945\",[\"B003\"]],[\"104:7:4\",\"root_001122\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ateşin yüreklere erişmesi, oraya yerleşmiş yanlış hesabı bulup açığa çıkaran bir iç denetime dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_cd174e11da8e6a2f57a8","text":"104:8 surprised this ayah by making the fire's access final: it reaches the hearts before enclosure closes over them. 104:9 added vertical and structural resonance; تطلع rises, and then عمد ممددة hold the enclosure in an extended vertical/linear form.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d2733fd8aa88caa622e9","text":"2. **A rising luminary inside the concealed person.** Evidence: `ط ل ع B001` (rising of a luminary) with `ن و ر B001/B002` and `و ق د B001` from 104:6. Structural cue: نار is followed by تطلع. Assigned role: طلوع turns the fire's motion into a kind of rising light. Abductive move: the heart becomes the horizon on which the divine fire rises. Reading change: the ayah joins punishment to illumination: the hidden interior is dawned upon.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e6c256a3265f82258500","text":"3. **A targeted access-route rather than surface burning.** Fixed `تَطَّلِعُ` activates `ط ل ع B006`, ascent, approach, or access-point, and fixed `ٱلْأَفْـِٔدَةِ` activates `ف ء د B003`, a heart struck or afflicted. The sequence from being cast into the Crusher (`104:4`), through kindling (`104:6`), to the heart, then sealing (`104:8`), supplies a one-way route. I infer that the fire uses the body or enclosure as an approach to a designated endpoint. The construction `عَلَى ٱلْأَفْـِٔدَةِ` therefore changes the fire from general atmosphere into a directed process that completes itself at the inward target.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001122/B001","root_001122/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e90dc2b0938ca86d7188","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:7:4\",\"root_001122\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ezici'nin tüketen ve kapalı işleyişinde yürek, dış kabuktan sonra sürekli ısıya uğrayan son madde hâline gelir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B001","root_000945/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ec6016de5ca438403fd7","text":"2. **A reversed dawn inside the person.** Fixed `تَطَّلِعُ` activates `ط ل ع B001`, the rising of a luminary, in proximity to `نَارُ`, whose `ن و ر B001` is illumination and `B002` is fire. Unlike an ordinary luminary rising over a horizon, this one rises over plural hearts. The packet supplies fire, rising, and the shifted spatial object; I infer the inversion of the usual outward scene. The ayah can consequently read as an inward dawn whose illumination exposes rather than reassures, with each heart functioning as the horizon of disclosure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f2df099f502d938fcd00","text":"`104:8` made the access activated by `ط ل ع B006` one-directional: the fire reaches inward before the enclosure is sealed over its occupants. `104:9` then supplied the extended structure that maintains that asymmetry. The correction is spatially important: `تَطَّلِعُ` is not retained as unrestricted rising, but as successful ingress to the fixed heart-target without a corresponding path of egress.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f579dae0ded07f77afac","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:7:2\",\"root_000945\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ateş, dış ufukta değil insanın içinde doğan; aydınlatması rahatlatmak yerine gizliyi açığa çıkaran tersine çevrilmiş bir şafak gibi belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
